(a2) **Sub-drag finger position is read from the GRAB HANDLE's own window pos** (`subHandleTops`, an `onGloballyPositioned` on the drag-handle modifier) since the pointer position is relative to that node — the old row-level top wasn't landing for **cycle** sub-rows (they sit inside the per-line `graphicsLayer` Column), so `crossTarget`/section detection stayed dead and **cycle sub-lines never showed a cross drop bar even though pinned sub-lines did**. (b) **Sub-line dragged fully out of the UI reverts** (back to its sub area) instead of promoting — promote (`onOutside`) now requires `outside && withinCard`; a drop outside the card entirely falls through to revert. (c) **Cycle main-line sub-0 visibility invariant (`repairCycleMainVisibility`)**: a cycle slide's MAIN sub-line (sub 0) must NEVER be sub-level `hidden` — its visibility is the line's mute (`cycleLineEnabled`), and the editor never exposes an eye for sub 0. A pinned↔cycle move used to carry a hidden flag onto sub 0, so the line decoded as blank → **uncounted in the "N lines" summary AND non-sending while still showing its text** (tester: "shows 10 lines but says 9", found via the saved preset). The repair force-unhides sub 0 on every slide; it's wired into the load path (`setCycleLinesFromTextPreserve` → self-heals on next open and re-persists the clean form, also healing the selected preset via the autosave) and the top of `persistCycleLinesPreserve` (every mutation's output). Source-side: `promoteCycleSubLine`/`movePinnedRowToCycle` map a hidden row to a **muted** line (`cycleLineEnabled = !hidden`, sub 0 visible) instead of a hidden main line; `moveCycleLineToPinned`/`moveCycleSubToPinned` force the pinned main row (row 0, which has no eye) visible.
  - **Increment 3 — cross-section Pinned↔Cycle**: `CompactSectionCard` gained an `expandedState` (hoisted) + `modifier` param; the Pinned & Cycle cards report their window rects (`boundsInWindow`) and `sectionAt(fingerY)`/`updateCross(origin, fingerY)` set `crossTarget` when the finger is over the OTHER section (which **auto-expands** + border-highlights; a cross INTO Pinned also force-expands its sub-rows via `expanded = pinnedExpanded || crossTarget == "pinned"` so every slot shows). On drop: **cycle line → Pinned** (`vm.moveCycleLineToPinned(from, at)`), **cycle sub → Pinned** (`vm.moveCycleSubToPinned(slide, sub, at)`), **pinned row → Cycle** (`vm.movePinnedRowToCycle(subIndex, at)`, becomes a new cycle line). Cross-section takes priority over demote/reorder on drop. All cross ops cap at 3 pinned rows / 20 cycle lines and no-op (revert) when they don't fit. Menu Move up/down/Duplicate/Delete kept as fallbacks throughout. **Drag GHOST overlay (`AutomationsPage`, replaced the in-place `translationY` float)**: the picked-up row used to float via `graphicsLayer{translationY}` INSIDE its card — but a card CLIPS its content and is a Z-sibling of the other card, so during a cross-section drag the pill drew BEHIND the other card, and an in-place offset DESYNCED from the finger whenever a card's layout shifted (the Pinned auto-expand). Now the real row **stays in its slot and just DIMS** (`alpha 0.4`, no translation), while a **display-only copy is rendered in a page-level overlay `Box` ABOVE every card** (`zIndex(1f)`, outside all clips — `AutomationsPage`'s root is now `Box { PageContainer{...}; ghost }`). The ghost is positioned by the **ABSOLUTE finger**: `offset = dragFingerY − dragGrabPointY − overlayWinTop` (y) and `ghostLeftPx − overlayWinLeft` (x), read inside the `.offset{}` lambda (layout-phase, no recomposition → smooth). `dragGrabPointY` is captured ONCE on the first drag frame (`fingerY − rowTop`), so any constant anchor error cancels and the finger stays locked to its exact grab point; because `dragFingerY` recomputes off the row's live window anchor each frame, a layout shift cancels too (no jump, no drift). Row window left/width are captured (`cycleRowLeft/Width`, `subRowLeft/Width`) so the ghost matches the row's exact bounds; the ghost re-renders `CycleLineRow`/`SubLineRow` (no handlers, `dragActive=true`) from `vm` + the field maps. The drop-math offset (`cycleDragOffsetY`/`subDragOffsetY`, which folds auto-scroll) and the insertion bars are UNCHANGED — only the visual moved to the overlay. This is what stopped the earlier attempts (raw `translationY=offset` → clipped + desync; `translationY = dragVisualTop − liveRowTop` → per-frame async jitter): the rows no longer move at all, so nothing can jitter, and the ghost tracks a single synchronously-updated finger value. **2-second hover-to-arm + cycle-sub promote bar**: cross-section and demote no longer trigger the instant the finger passes over a target — they require a **2s continuous hold** (`pendingCross`/`pendingDemote` hold the instant hover; a `LaunchedEffect(pending…)` promotes it to the armed `crossTarget`/`cycleDemoteTarget` after `delay(2000)`, and the key-change restarts the wait so dragging PAST never arms). Arming a demote expands **only that specific cycle line's subs** (`cycleExpanded[t]=true`, per-line, not the whole card); `fingerOverCycleLine` keeps the armed target sticky so the expansion's layout shift can't disarm it. A **cycle SUB dragged OUT of its block** into the main cycle flow now shows the SAME insertion bar the cycle reorder uses (`subPromoteIndex` = `crossDropBoundary("cycle", fingerY)`, drawn in the cycle-Column `drawBehind`; the within-block bar is suppressed while promoting) and `promoteCycleSubLine(slide, sub, at)` inserts the new line at that index. **Nest-into-a-line for ALL source types**: hovering ANY dragged item over a cycle line's CENTRE band (2s) nests it as a **sub-line of that line** — cycle line→line is the existing `demoteCycleLineInto`; a **cycle sub** → `moveCycleSubIntoLine(fromSlide, sub, target)` and a **pinned row/sub** → `movePinnedRowIntoCycleLine(pinnedIndex, target)` are the new analogues (both append the row, `canNestIntoCycleLine` gates the 3-row cap, `persistCycleLinesPreserve` self-heals main visibility). Detection is finger-based (`cycleLineCenterUnderFinger(fingerY, exclude)`, excludes a cycle sub's own parent); the nest is armed by the SAME 2s `pendingDemote`→`cycleDemoteTarget` effect and expands only that line. For a pinned source over the cycle area, a centre-hover **nest wins over** the plain cross-to-new-line insert (`if (pendingDemote != null) pendingCross = null`); the target line shows the green/red validity tint (`cycleDemoteValid` extended: `canDemoteCycleInto` for a line source, `canNestIntoCycleLine` for a sub/pinned source). `subDragHandle` gained an `onNest` callback threaded to those VM ops. **Positional cross-drop + indicator**: the cross ops take an `at` insertion index (append when `< 0`); `updateCross` computes `crossDropIndex` from the finger vs the TARGET section's row midpoints (`crossDropBoundary`), and the target section draws the SAME bright insertion bar it uses for in-section reorder (added to the cycle-Column `drawBehind` when `crossTarget == "cycle"` and to `subRowMod`'s `drawBehind` for the pinned block when `crossTarget == "pinned"`) — so you can see WHERE the item lands across sections, not just that a cross is armed. The SOURCE list suppresses its own reorder bar while `crossTarget != null` (the item's bar shows in the other section). **Edge/overshoot tolerance (the "first/last line reverts if dragged a tiny bit too far" fix)**: the old revert test keyed on the dragged row's list-relative `curTop` vs a tight `draggedH * 0.8/0.9` margin, so a small over-drag past the first/last row reverted. Both the cycle-line and sub-line drops now accept a reorder (clamped to the nearest slot via `cycleDropTarget`/`subDropTarget`) as long as the finger is still within the section's card rect + `DRAG_ACCEPT_MARGIN` (72dp) — tracked via `dragFingerY` set each `onDrag`; only a drop COMPLETELY outside the card reverts. Cycle-sub PROMOTE (drag clearly out of the block) keeps its own `outMargin` (raised to `draggedH * 1.4f` so a tiny overshoot reorders instead of promoting).
- **Cycle line system (revamp + features)** (`AutomationsPage.kt` `CycleLineRow` + `VrcaViewModel`): line cap raised **10 → 20** (`VrcaViewModel.MAX_CYCLE_LINES`). Each line is a slim bordered `Surface` (~44dp) on `BasicTextField` with: an inline **number badge that doubles as the mute toggle** (dims when muted) + a `Visibility`/`VisibilityOff` mute icon, an **always-on** per-line `CharBudgetMeter`, and a `MoreVert` **overflow menu** (Move up / Move down / Duplicate / Delete). The line currently being SENT is highlighted live (primary tint + border) via a 1s ticker reading `vm.cycleActiveRawIndex()`. **New VM capabilities**: per-line mute (`cycleLineEnabled` — a `mutableStateListOf<Boolean>` kept index-aligned with `cycleLines`; LOCAL-only, persisted as a `1`/`0` CSV in DataStore `cycle_line_enabled`, NOT synced to Firestore — a muted line stays in the editor + the synced `cycleLinesText` but the sender skips it); `duplicateCycleLine`/`moveCycleLine`/`setCycleLineEnabled`; **shuffle** (`cycleShuffle` / `setCycleShuffleFlag`, DataStore `cycle_shuffle`, local-only) — random rotation via `nextCyclePos()` with a **no-repeat window** (`shuffleWindow(activeSize)`: how many recently-played positions to avoid before a line can recur — `1` for <5 active lines (just no immediate repeat), then ramps by linear interpolation between anchors **5→3, 10→5, 15→8, 20→14** (clamped above 20), so more lines enforce more variety; the window is computed over the **active** line count — blank AND muted lines are excluded by `activeCycleLines()`, so empty slots never count); **dynamic tokens** resolved live at send/preview time by the PUBLIC `resolveTokens()` (renamed from `resolveCycleTokens`) — `{time}` (Time-line string), `{song}` ("Artist - Title" now playing), `{world}` (VRChat world name), `{players}` (instance "n/cap"). **Tokens work in BOTH Pinned and Cycle** — `buildCombinedText` runs the `afkLine` through `resolveTokens` too. The sender (`startCycle`) + preview (`currentCycleLinePreview`) + idle ticker (`tickCyclePreviewOnly`) all walk `activeCycleLines()` (non-blank, non-muted, capped) and resolve tokens. The **char meter counts the RESOLVED length** (`vm.resolveTokens(text).length`) in both editors, so a `{world}` shows the actual world name's character cost, not the literal `{world}`. The hint line under both editors reads "Currently experimenting, feel free to try by putting these tags into your pinned and cycle message: {time} {song} {world} {players}" (shared `TokensHint()`). `setCycleLinesFromTextPreserve` is change-aware (an echo of our own `saveCycleMessages` keeps the local mute/shuffle state; only a genuine line-set change resets mute). Cycle speed chips (2s/5s/10s/20s/40s) unchanged. **Eye/mute responsiveness (hard-won — SIX prior attempts failed)**: the mute flags are snapshotted in the PAGE scope (`cycleEnabledFlags = vm.cycleLineEnabled.toList()`) and each `CycleLineRow` is wrapped in **`key(idx, lineEn)`** so a mute toggle forces Compose to recreate the row outright. Reading the state inside the `CompactSectionCard` content lambda, passing a provider lambda, or passing a changed plain Boolean WITHOUT the key() all failed on-device — the `AnimatedVisibility` content slot skipped the rows even when their params changed (the header summary, read in page scope, updated instantly the whole time). Do not remove the key() wrapper. **Focus-expand**: a focused cycle line field flips `singleLine=false`/`maxLines=6` (via `onFocusChanged`) so a long line wraps and is fully visible while typing, collapsing back to one line on blur. **Cursor-teleport fix (Pinned + Cycle editors)**: the fields are local `TextFieldValue` maps (`cycleLineFields` keyed `"slide:sub"`, `pinnedFields` keyed by index) synced FROM the VM via `syncCycleLineFieldsFromVm`/`syncPinnedFieldsFromVm`. The old sync rebuilt `cycleLineFields[k] = TextFieldValue(s.text)` whenever the round-tripped text differed — and `TextFieldValue(text)` defaults its selection to `TextRange.Zero` (the FRONT), so a keystroke's own VM echo yanked the cursor to position 0 mid-typing (tester: "cursor teleports to the front/randomly when typing or deleting fast"). Two guards: (1) a shared `focusedFieldKey` (`"c:$slide:$sub"` / `"p:$idx"`, set by each field's `onFocusChanged` → `onFieldFocus`, threaded through `CycleLineRow`/`SubLineRow`/`SubLineEditor` `onFocusChange` params) makes the sync SKIP the field the user is actively editing — its own echo can never clobber the live cursor; external changes (preset load, admin edit, cross-device) apply on blur. (2) `reseedField(existing, text)` PRESERVES the cursor (clamped) instead of resetting to `TextRange.Zero`, and returns the same reference when text is unchanged (no no-op state write) — the fallback for any non-focused re-seed. Manual Send (`vm.messageText`) already preserves the cursor (only scroll-mode forces end, intentional); admin fields use plain local `String` state (synchronous, unaffected).
- **Preset auto-save model (Pinned + Cycle)** (`VrcaViewModel` `selectedAfkPreset`/`selectedCyclePreset` + `AutomationsPage`): the SELECTED slot is the auto-save target — editing the Pinned text / cycle lines writes straight into the selected slot, so **switching slots IS the only "save" (no manual save button)**. **Slot 0 = NOTHING selected is the DEFAULT (DataStore `selected_afk_preset`/`selected_cycle_preset` default 0)** — `autoSaveSelectedAfkPreset` (`if (selectedAfkPreset !in 1..3) return`) and `autoSaveSelectedCyclePreset` (`if (selectedCyclePreset !in 1..5) return`) NO-OP when nothing is selected, so editing touches NO preset until the user deliberately taps a slot. **This is the backward-compat guarantee**: existing users (no DataStore key → 0) never have a saved preset clobbered by editing; the old unsafe default of 1 would overwrite slot 1 on the first edit. **Slot-0 data-loss migration (`migrateLiveContentIntoPresetSlot`, one-shot via DataStore `preset_seed_migrated`)**: slot 0 protects presets from EDITS but not from SELECTS — a pre-update user's live Pinned message / cycle lines are saved in NO slot, so their first tap on a preset chip loaded that slot over the live editor and silently destroyed their content. On VM init (after the initial DataStore load, before `initialDataLoaded`), the migration parks the live content in a slot once: prefer a slot already holding identical content (select it, no write), else the first EMPTY slot (save + select). All-slots-full-with-distinct-content leaves slot 0 and changes nothing (never clobber a deliberate save — accepted edge). Selecting during migration does NOT reload the editor (content is identical by construction). Fresh installs just get their default message parked in slot 1 — harmless. **Stale preset chips/peek fix (`AutomationsPage`)**: preset previews + the selected slot are snapshotted in the PAGE scope (`afkPresetPreviews`/`cyclePresetPreviews` Lists) — reads inside a `CompactSectionCard` content lambda don't reliably retrigger its `AnimatedVisibility` (same class of bug as the cycle-mute rows), so the auto-save writing into the selected slot left the chips and long-press peek stale until the tab was re-entered. Chips are wrapped in `key(slot, preview, equipped)` to force the repaint through, and `onPeek` reads the content FRESH from the VM at press time instead of using the composition-captured value. `updateAfkText` → `autoSaveSelectedAfkPreset`; every cycle content edit (`persistCycleLinesPreserve`) → `autoSaveSelectedCyclePreset`. **Per-preset hide state (`cycle_pN_enabled`)**: each cycle preset stores its OWN per-line mute CSV (aligned to the preset's NON-EMPTY saved lines, so indices survive the empty-line drop in `messages`) via `cyclePresetEnabled` + the new `enabledCsv` param on `saveCyclePresetN`. This fixed "presets don't save hidden lines" — the hide state used to live ONLY in the global `cycle_line_enabled` CSV, which `setCycleLinesFromTextPreserve` reset to all-visible on every preset load, so a preset never remembered which lines were muted. **`setCycleLineEnabled` (the mute toggle) now DOES auto-save the selected preset** (`autoSaveSelectedCyclePreset()`, no-op at slot 0) — reversing the earlier "mute toggles don't auto-save" design, which was the root cause. `selectCyclePreset` restores the slot's hide CSV after loading its lines (a BLANK CSV — legacy presets saved before this feature — explicitly resets to all-visible so a switch between identical-line presets can't inherit the prior slot's mute). `saveCyclePreset(slot, lines)` compat helper and the cross-device/admin apply lambdas pass no `enabledCsv` (defaults to "" = all-visible; the CSV is local-only, never synced to Firestore). `selectAfkPreset(slot)`/`selectCyclePreset(slot)` record the new selection (1..N) and load that slot's content into the live editor (loads, never clears). The preset chips highlight by `slot == vm.selectedAfkPreset/selectedCyclePreset` (no content-match; nothing highlighted at slot 0). The long-press peek dialog is view-only now (Close + "Switch to this preset"); the "Save current here" button is gone. The old `saveAfkPreset`/`saveCyclePreset`/`loadAfkPreset`/`loadCyclePreset` helpers are now unused by the UI (kept for compat).
- **Onboarding flow** (`ui/onboarding/OnboardingFlow.kt`): first-open tutorial pager (takes the app-scoped `vm` — `OnboardingFlow(vm, …)`), 8 steps in FINAL order — Welcome+ToS (hard gate, embeds the redesigned `TosGate`) → VRChat login (hard gate, embeds `VrchatLoginScreen`, shows a **platform-status warning** from a one-shot `status.vrchat.com/api/v2/summary.json` fetch since the pipeline's status poll only runs post-login) → Permissions (POST_NOTIFICATIONS runtime, Notification Access, battery exemption, **Install updates** (`canRequestPackageInstalls` / `ACTION_MANAGE_UNKNOWN_APP_SOURCES` — needed for in-app APK updates), **Overlay (optional)**; statuses re-checked on `ON_RESUME`; Samsung "Never sleeping apps" note. **POST_NOTIFICATIONS is requested ONLY here / in Settings — `MainActivity` no longer fires the runtime prompt on first open**; KeepAliveService starts regardless, its notification just stays hidden until granted. A collapsed `CompactSectionCard` ("Turn off background restrictions", `persistExpansion=false`) explains long-press icon → App info → 3-dot menu → "Allow background activity"/Unrestricted, and to keep VRC-A out of any restricted/sleeping-apps list — the Samsung note nests inside it. A SECOND collapsed card at the TOP of the step ("A permission won't turn on?", `persistExpansion=false`) explains the **"Allow restricted settings"** gateway: Android 13+ blocks SIDELOADED apps from granting Notification Access / Accessibility (the switch is greyed out / shows "Restricted setting") until the user opens App info → 3-dot menu → "Allow restricted settings". Placed first so it's seen when a toggle won't enable — this is the real reason a permission appears ungrantable on a non-Play-Store install) → IP entry (**auto-saves what you type** — a 500ms-debounced `LaunchedEffect(ip)` writes slot 1 + active IP via repo, so NO button press is needed to persist; the button is now an OPTIONAL "Check reachability" `pingHost` test only, per user complaint that users didn't realise they had to click to save. The typed IP becomes the **live OSC target** automatically because `VrcaViewModel.init` now collects `userPreferencesRepository.ipAddress` into `remoteVrcaOsc.ipAddress` — previously the app-scoped VM, constructed during onboarding before any IP existed, kept sending to `127.0.0.1` until the user manually tapped Apply in Home. The "Good to know" card on this step is a collapsed `CompactSectionCard` (`persistExpansion=false`) so the IP input sits in view without scrolling) → Enable OSC (numbered instructions with the 4 `osc_tutorial_N` TutorialImage frames, all on the single step; **the images are downloaded on-demand — see `TutorialImageStore` below — not bundled**) → Notification types (**the FULL per-type `NotificationToggleSection(vm)` — the same 27-toggle expandable UI as Settings**, replacing the old 5 master section switches, so first-time users pick and choose every type; toggles persist via the VM/DataStore directly) → Discord RPC (consent checkbox → `DiscordLoginWebView`; sets risk/seeded/enabled flags) → Test message finale (scoped `VrcaOsc(ip,port)` loop sends "VRC-A connected ✓" every 2s while active, empty-send clears on stop/exit; deliberately bypasses the Start/Stop gate — no toggles touched, `FeatureSessionStore` never armed). **The test step is a HARD PING GATE** (non-replay): Finish stays hidden until `testDone`, and `testDone` flips ONLY when the saved IP answers a ping — while the test runs, `pingHost` attempts repeat every 3s and the step passes the moment one succeeds; there is deliberately **NO manual "it showed up" bypass** (per the user: a wrong IP must be fixed, not waved through). A troubleshooting card (same Wi-Fi / right IP / headset awake / OSC enabled) with a "Fix the address" jump-back shows while waiting. **`pingHost(address)`** (public, in `OnboardingFlow.kt`) is the robust reachability check: `InetAddress.isReachable` first (often fails on Android — no raw ICMP socket), then the system `/system/bin/ping -c 1` binary fallback; the Home Connection card's 20s reachability chip uses it too so the chip and the tutorial gate agree. No saved IP → a "Go to the address step" button jumps back to step 4 (`onGoToIpStep`). **No em dashes in any tutorial-visible string** (user said they look bad — also swept `VrchatLoginScreen`'s copy, which the tutorial embeds; don't reintroduce them in onboarding/login UI text). **Footer**: a "Step N of 8" label + animated progress pills (current step is an elongated 24dp pill via `animateDpAsState`, visited pills are tappable to jump back). **The flow root clears text-field focus on any empty-space tap** (`detectTapGestures` → `LocalFocusManager.clearFocus()` — a parent gesture detector only sees taps children didn't claim); the same pattern lives in the main app's `PageContainer` (`VrcaScreen.kt`) so every tab dismisses a stuck focused field/keyboard. The IP step's Quest instructions are 4 expanded numbered steps + a "Good to know" card (IPv6 warning, same-Wi-Fi, IP-changes note, PC ipconfig path) — written to stand alone since Quest Settings screenshots are DRM-blanked. Gating in `VrcaApp`: `OnboardingPrefs` (`vrca_onboarding` prefs, `complete` + resume `step`); **pre-seeded complete when `TosPrefs.acceptedVersion > 0`** so existing installs never see it. **Replay**: Settings → App → "Replay setup tutorial" sets `OnboardingState.replayRequested` (process-level mutableStateOf); replay renders hard gates as completed checkmark cards and has an "Exit tutorial" button. Phase-2 ban check + pipeline start still run via the normal post-gate logic in `VrcaApp` (the login-gate `remember` first executes after onboarding returns, so it correctly reads the now-logged-in state).
- **On-demand OSC tutorial images (`ui/onboarding/TutorialImageStore.kt`)**: the 4 `osc_tutorial_N.png` instruction images are **NOT bundled in the APK** — they were ~617 KB for a screen most users see once, so shipping them was pure bloat. They're downloaded on-demand from the shared image-store repo (`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Ashoska/VRC-A-Image-store/main/osc_tutorial_N.png` — the SAME repo, renamed from `VRChat-rpc-display`, that hosts the Discord RPC default image `vrchat-1102x620.jpg`; `DiscordRpcService.DEFAULT_VRCHAT_IMAGE_URL` was updated to the new name in the same change) into `filesDir/tutorial_images/` and deleted the moment the tutorial ends. `ensureDownloaded(ctx)` is idempotent (skips cached, `.part`-file-then-rename so a partial download never looks complete); `clear(ctx)` deletes them. **`TutorialImage(index, …)` (PublicUiKit)** resolves the local file, shows a `CircularProgressIndicator` while it downloads, renders via Coil (`AsyncImage` inline + `rememberAsyncImagePainter` in the pinch-zoom dialog), and calls `ensureDownloaded` itself as a fallback. **Lifecycle (the "no stale images" design)**: (1) **New-user boot** — `VrcaApp`'s bootstrap, for a logged-out user whose onboarding is `!isComplete`, spends the boot-screen time PREFETCHING the images (`withTimeoutOrNull(TUTORIAL_PREFETCH_CAP_MS = 8s)`) instead of the old fixed fake wait, then still honors the `NEW_USER_BOOT_FLOOR_MS` floor so the screen doesn't flash / the cached case doesn't skip past onboarding; (2) **Replay** — `OnboardingFlow` re-downloads on entry (`LaunchedEffect(Unit) { ensureDownloaded }`, idempotent); (3) **Tutorial complete OR replay exit** — `VrcaApp`'s `onFinish` (the single convergence point for both first-run completion via `advance()` and replay's "Exit tutorial" via `finishNow()`) calls `TutorialImageStore.clear(ctx)`; (4) **A new user who closes/kills mid-onboarding KEEPS the images** — onboarding is still `!complete`, so no cleanup runs and boot re-prefetches any missing → resume works; (5) **A replay killed mid-way** leaves strays, so `VrcaApplication.onCreate` runs `clearStaleTutorialImages()` on a fresh process — if onboarding is already `complete` (i.e. not a genuine new user), it deletes the dir (replay state is process-level `OnboardingState.replayRequested`, always false at a cold process start, so a killed replay self-cleans). Net: images exist only while a tutorial is actually in progress; the APK ships zero tutorial images.
- **ToS gate redesign** (`VrcaApp.kt`): `parseTosBlocks()` converts the admin-configurable plain text into styled blocks — numbered headers (`^\d+\. `) render primary-colored (error-colored for Risk/Moderation/Acceptable Use sections), `*`/`-` lines become real bullets, literal markdown links `[x](y)` are collapsed by `sanitizeTosLine`. Layout: sticky title + version pill header, scrollable body, pinned accept bar with a **Checkbox** (was a Switch). `TosGate` is `internal` so onboarding embeds it.
- **Update dialog redesign** (`VrcaApp.kt`): patch notes render through the same `StyledBlocks` parser inside a **scrollable area capped at ~45% screen height** (long notes used to clip the dialog). Header = title + version pill. Forced variant (the default — all releases forced) = single full-width Download button; Later only on the legacy non-forced path. Browser-fallback link kept.
- **Boot loading screen (`BootstrapScreen`, `VrcaApp.kt`)**: gradient background (theme background fading into a faint primary tint via `lerp`), the dedicated high-res logo (`R.drawable.vrca_logo`, `drawable-nodpi`, a 512px copy of the chibi launcher art — NOT the small `@mipmap/ic_launcher`, which would upscale and blur at this ~136dp size) clipped circular in a tonal circle with a gentle breathing `scale` pulse while working, "VRC-A / VRChat Assistant" title block, a **3-row boot checklist** in order **Device session → VRChat systems → Account status** (`BootCheckRow` — per-row states pending·spinner·green-✓·amber-!·grey-?), a lifetime stat line, and a `v{VERSION_NAME}` footer pinned bottom-center. (The earlier rotating "DID YOU KNOW" `BOOT_TIPS` card was REMOVED per the user — do not re-add.) Takes a `phase` param (1 = bootstrap, 2 = phase-1 ban check) instead of a status string; the ban-check gate reuses the SAME screen so the whole boot reads as one continuous loading experience. **No fake wait**: the old fixed 5s `BOOT_MIN_DURATION_MS` hold was removed. Instead, during the **Device session** stage, if `VrchatAuthManager.isLoggedIn(ctx)`, `startPipelineWarmup(ctx)` fires the pipeline foreground service so **VRChat presence starts connecting in parallel with the rest of boot** (idempotent with the normal post-login start — the service coalesces a duplicate `ACTION_START`). **Account status now represents the ban/moderation check (`checkPhase1`) AND VRChat presence readiness**: for a logged-in user it also `withTimeoutOrNull(PRESENCE_WARM_CAP_MS = 5s)`-waits for `VrchatPipelineState.isConnectedFlow.first { it }` before the row goes green (`accountReady = phase1Checked && presenceReady`), so by the time it's reached the pipeline is "pretty much good"; the cap guarantees a dead session / bad network never hangs boot (it just hands off). A **logged-out / new user** has no presence to warm — `presenceReady` starts true — so Account status is just the ban check, and a `NEW_USER_BOOT_FLOOR_MS = 3s` floor holds the screen so it doesn't flash by before onboarding. **VRChat systems row**: one-shot fetch of `status.vrchat.com/api/v2/summary.json` via `fetchBootVrcStatus()` into the process-level `BootVrcStatus` holder (`AtomicBoolean` fetch guard — BootstrapScreen is composed twice per boot and must not refetch/flicker); never blocks boot. **Lifetime stat line** ("You've sent N chatbox updates", `%,d` formatted): from `ChatboxStats` (`com.vrca.app`, `vrca_stats` SharedPreferences) — incremented at the `VrcaOsc.sendOscMessage` chokepoint for every non-blank `/chatbox/input` send (typing signals + blank clears excluded), in-memory `AtomicLong` flushed every 25 sends, attached in `VrcaApplication.onCreate`. Hidden until ≥100 so fresh installs never see "0". Boot screen ONLY — deliberately no Settings stats block. The startup-error card + Retry behavior is unchanged. **Cold-start anon-auth retry (`bootstrapFirebaseAndCache`)**: the "Device session" row's only hard network dependency is `FirebaseAuth.signInAnonymously().await()` (the rest of bootstrap is `runCatching`/non-fatal). On a brand-new install's FIRST open the network + Firebase stack is often still cold, so a single attempt failed transiently → the row showed the error state and the user had to reopen (by which point it was warm and the anon credential cached) — the "Device session sometimes fails on first open" report. Fix: a bounded retry (up to 4 attempts, exponential backoff 400ms→3s, worst case ~5.8s, well within the 20s outer `withTimeout`) so a cold-start blip self-heals inside the boot window instead of surfacing a manual-retry error. **Boot screen shows ONLY on a clean swipe-reopen or first install (the "boot screen shows every time the app is brought back after an OEM/force kill, not just on a real open" fix)**: the boot screen exists to cover a genuine, deliberate cold start (Firebase anon sign-in + device hash + pipeline warm-up). But its gate state (`bootOk`/`phase1Checked`/`presenceReady`/`phase1HoldDone`) was all Activity-scoped `remember`, so ANY new Activity composition re-ran the whole sequence and re-showed the screen — including reopening after an OEM/force kill, a headless revival, or a plain Activity re-create, where everything is already set up. **The decision now mirrors how the Quick Toggles / `FeatureSessionStore` restore tell a deliberate swipe apart from an OS/OEM kill: the persistent `swiped_away` flag** (`AppShutdown.isSwipedAway()`, set ONLY by `onTaskSwiped`, which an OS/OEM/force kill never runs). `MainActivity.onCreate` captures **`VrcaApplication.openedFromSwipe = AppShutdown.isSwipedAway(ctx)` at the TOP, BEFORE `clearSwipedAway()` wipes it**. `VrcaApp` computes `val warmResume = !(openedFromSwipe || !everBootstrapped)` where `everBootstrapped` = a non-blank cached `auth_uid` in `vrca_remote` prefs (bootstrap writes it and it persists), and SEEDS all four boot-gate booleans from `warmResume`. So the boot screen shows **only** on (a) a clean **swipe**-reopen (a deliberate fresh start) or (b) a true **first install / data clear** (no cached auth uid → bootstrap MUST run to sign in anonymously + cache the uid the ban check reads); **every other relaunch — OEM/force kill, Activity recreate, headless revival — is a recovery and drops straight into the app with no boot screen.** Skipping boot on a swipe-less relaunch is safe because the auth uid + device hash already persist in prefs and the VM's `ensureAnonAuth` re-signs-in if the cached Firebase session lapsed. The ban-check `LaunchedEffect(bootOk)` still fires once on a warm resume (its key is true from the first composition) so `phase1BanId` is still populated and the downstream ban gate works — the UI just isn't blocked on it; `startPipelineWarmup`/`bootstrapFirebaseAndCache` are correctly skipped on warm resume (the VM/services own pipeline start + liveness). (The earlier `runtimeVmAlive` attempt was abandoned — `MainActivity` always starts `KeepAliveService` → `ensureRuntimeViewModel()` on EVERY open, which flipped that flag true and raced the composition, producing the inverted "swipe-reopen skips boot but everything else shows it" behavior; the swipe flag is unaffected by the service start.)
- **Backend-name sanitization (`sanitizeBackendRefs`, `VrcaApp.kt`)**: every USER-VISIBLE error/debug string is scrubbed of backend-implementation names before display (replaces `com.google.firebase…`/`com.google.android.gms…` class paths with "backend" and any `Firebase*`/`Firestore*` token with "server") so exception text can't reveal the server stack to users (exploit-surface hygiene, per the user). Applied at: the boot `bootError`, the `CrashScreen` crash-text display (stored log stays raw for debugging), and Settings → Debug ("Sync (last issue)" — renamed from "Firebase (last issue)" — and the moderation-listener error). Logs keep raw text. Route any NEW user-facing error surface through this; never hardcode "Firebase"/"Firestore" in public-build UI strings (admin build is exempt — not distributed).
- **Settings Accounts + VRChat-logout OSC gate (Phase 3)**: `VrcaSettingsPage` gains an **Accounts** section (VRChat row: display name + Sign out with confirm dialog → `VrchatAuthManager.logout`; signed-out state shows "Sign in" → full-screen `VrchatLoginScreen` takeover with Cancel. Discord row: status from `discordSessionSeeded`/`discordRpcEnabled` + Disconnect with confirm, mirroring the VRChat-tab disconnect logic). **OSC hard-block while signed out of VRChat**: `VrcaViewModel.startVrchatAuthGateWatcher()` (init, app-scoped VM) collects `VrchatAuthManager.loggedOutSignal` → `stopSending()` FIRST (so the chatbox-clearing send escapes before the block) then sets `vrchatLoggedOut=true`; `refreshOscBlockGate()` ORs `forceUpdatePending || vrchatLoggedOut` into `VrcaOsc.blocked` (both gates share the chokepoint without clobbering each other — `applyForceUpdateGate` also routes through it). **`VrchatAuthManager.loggedInSignal`** (new SharedFlow, emitted on Basic-auth Success + 2FA verify Success) lifts the gate; re-login never auto-starts sending. **There is NO forced login screen anywhere anymore** — the only hard login gate is the onboarding tutorial's login step on first run. `VrcaApp`'s standalone `if (!vrcLoginDone) VrchatLoginScreen` gate was DELETED: a cold start while signed out lands straight in the app with OSC blocked (per the user: "not being logged in should just make it so osc doesnt work"). To make that safe, **`vrchatLoggedOut` is seeded from the REAL auth state** (`!VrchatAuthManager.isLoggedIn(app)`) instead of `false`, and `startVrchatAuthGateWatcher()` calls `refreshOscBlockGate()` immediately — without this a signed-out cold start would have an OPEN gate until a `loggedOutSignal` that never fires. Sign-in surfaces: Settings → Accounts and the **VRChat tab** (`VrchatStatusPage` now owns its own `showLogin` full-screen `VrchatLoginScreen` takeover with Cancel — the old `onOpenLogin` param was wired to an EMPTY lambda in `VrcaScreen`, which was the "clicking login does nothing" bug; the param is gone). `VrchatStatusPage.isLinked` is now DERIVED from `vm.vrchatLoggedOut && isLoggedIn` (the old one-shot `remember { mutableStateOf(...) }` froze the cold-start value, so a Settings sign-out never updated the VRChat tab). Each re-login bumps `reloginTick`, which re-keys the Phase-2 ban check effect (a re-login could be a different, banned VRChat account) and restarts the pipeline; on a signed-out cold start the Phase-2 effect simply early-returns until `loggedInSignal` flips `vrcLoginDone`. **Notification toggles moved**: `NotificationToggleSection` now renders in Settings → Notifications (removed from the VRChat tab). **Storage row** (Settings → App): measures `cacheDir`+`externalCacheDir`+`codeCacheDir` on IO and deep-links to the system App Info page (apps can't fully wipe their own cache; the system Clear Cache button is the honest route — it never touches logins/settings, and `app_webview` lives in the DATA dir handled by the Discord 20 MB cap).
- **OSC auth-dead gate (confirmed-dead VRChat session)**: `isLoggedIn()` only checks the auth cookie is PRESENT, not valid — so a session that silently died (VRChat **password change**, **30-day trusted-device expiry**, cleared creds) left OSC working even though the user is effectively logged out (bad, since admins need users staying logged into VRChat). This gate stops OSC when the session is **confirmed dead-and-unrecoverable**, while tolerating transient issues. Detection lives in `VrchatPipelineService.evaluateSessionHealthOnReconnect()`, hooked into the existing reconnect path (`reconnectAttempt >= 2`, where a dead cookie surfaces as repeated WS failures — replaces the old inline `validateSession`+`autoRelogin`). It **never blames the environment**: guards return early (not dead) when the device lacks **validated** internet (`hasValidatedNetwork()`, `NET_CAPABILITY_VALIDATED` like the Discord RPC) or VRChat is in a **major/critical outage** (`isVrchatMajorOutage()` reads `VrchatPipelineState.statusPageState.indicator`). With a healthy environment it calls the new **tri-state `VrchatAuthManager.validateSessionDetailed()` → `SessionValidity` {VALID, UNAUTHORIZED, UNKNOWN}** (`GET /auth`: 200=VALID, 401=UNAUTHORIZED, 5xx/network/no-cookie=UNKNOWN — so "couldn't reach VRChat" is never "dead"); on `UNAUTHORIZED` it tries `autoRelogin()` — success clears, failure (2FA-required / bad creds / no saved creds) is the dead signal. A **`AUTH_DEAD_CONFIRM_MS` = 5-min window** (`authDeadSinceMs` timestamp, `markAuthDeadCandidate()`) must elapse with the state persisting before it flips `VrchatPipelineState.authDead=true` — rides out blips/brief outages. **The WS `onOpen` clears it** (`clearAuthDead()`): a successful handshake means the cookie is valid, so authDead can only ever be true while disconnected. `VrcaViewModel` collects `authDeadFlow` → `vrchatAuthDead` → `refreshOscBlockGate()` now ORs `forceUpdatePending || vrchatLoggedOut || accountDenied || vrchatAuthDead`; `stopSending()` fires first (chatbox-clearing send escapes before the block); `loggedInSignal` clears both `vrchatAuthDead` and `VrchatPipelineState.authDead`. **In-app only, NO notification** (per the user): a `VrchatSessionExpiredBanner` (`PublicUiKit`) shows on **Home** (button → navigate to the VRChat tab) and the **VRChat tab** (button → its `showLogin` takeover) — short copy naming the causes ("...after a password change or if VRC-A hasn't been opened in about 30 days. Sign in again to fix it."). Non-destructive: nothing is wiped; a successful sign-in clears the gate and lifts the block automatically (never auto-starts sending).

### Navigation
- Bottom nav: Home, Automations, **Media** (formerly "Music" — renamed since the NowPlaying block now covers YouTube videos too; enum constant stays `AppPage.Music`, only the display label/`title` changed), VRChat (4 items)
- **Automations (Phase 5 revamp, `AutomationsPage.kt`)**: the inner Pinned/Cycle `TabRow` is GONE (`ChatboxAutomationsTab` enum deleted from `VrcaScreen.kt`) — both features stack as `CompactSectionCard`s with live collapsed summaries (Pinned: the message text + ON/OFF `KitStatusChip`; Cycle: "5 lines · 10s · now: '…'" — the `now:` part uses the new public `vm.cycleCurrentLine()` and shows only while sending). Presets are a horizontally-scrolling **chip row** (`PresetChip`): tap = load/equip, long-press = peek dialog (full content via the new `vm.getCyclePresetFull(slot)`, with Load + "Save current here" buttons — Save lives ONLY in the peek dialog now); the equipped chip is highlighted by content-match (no "Selected" label). Editors have an inline **`CharBudgetMeter`** ("87/144", amber >114, red >144; mirrors the private `VRC_MAX_CHARS`) — always on for Pinned, per-line only past 100 chars for Cycle. Cycle's 10-field line editor + speed dropdown are unchanged.
- **VRChat tab (Phase 7 revamp, `VrchatPage.kt`)**: the two stacked identity cards (connection status + presence) are MERGED into one header `ElevatedCard`: `SelfAvatar` (the user's OWN VRChat+ pic via Coil through `VrchatImageLoader` — it attaches THIS device's session cookie, so it works on the public build too; initial-circle fallback; status dot pinned bottom-end) + name + colored status text + the user's status DESCRIPTION verbatim (e.g. "stars :0" — it's their VRChat status text, do NOT "fix" it) + a chips row (platform `Badge`, trust-rank `Badge` from the new `VrcUserPresence.trustRank` field — `fetchPresence` now extracts the highest `system_trust_*` tag from `/auth/user` + `/users/{id}` — and a small "RPC ●" chip when `discordSessionSeeded`, green dot when CONNECTED) + the world line (name + player count) + **friends online count** ("N of M friends online", `Icons.Filled.Group`). Sign out (error-tinted `Logout` icon) and View Profile (`OpenInNew`) are small trailing `IconButton`s. **Friends online count is FREE**: `VrchatPipelineState.friendsOnlineFlow` (`Pair(online,total)?`) is published by `VrchatPipelineService.publishFriendsOnline()` from the in-memory `friendsCache` on every cache mutation (`persistFriendsCache`) + restore — zero API calls. **Online predicate is STATUS-primary (over-count fix)**: `status` is authoritative — `"offline"` ⇒ offline, any other non-blank value (active/join me/ask me/busy) ⇒ online; `location` is consulted ONLY when `status` is blank (WS friend-online/friend-location payloads don't always carry `user.status`, so a live world location proves online then). **`location="offline"` is NOT an offline signal on its own** — VRChat uses it for a friend who is online on the WEBSITE but not in any world, so keying offline off location would drop website-active friends. This replaced an older `status-OR-location` predicate that counted a friend online if EITHER field looked online: a friend-update setting `status="offline"` while leaving a STALE world in `location` (VRChat often updates only one field) kept them counted → the "friends online way higher than actual, reopening fixes it" bug. **The confirmed `friend-offline` handler now marks the cache entry `status="offline"/location="offline"`** (after the flap cooldown) — previously the entry kept its stale online status forever, which would have frozen the count. **The in-app alerts header gains filter chips** (All / Friends / Groups / Bio — `alertMatchesFilter` keyed on alert-group-id prefixes: `announcement_`/`event_` = Groups, `bio_` = Bio, rest = Friends). **The Discord RPC card and the duplicated About/trust card are DELETED from this tab** — full Discord management (status line, enable `ToggleRow`, sign-in/sign-in-again, Disconnect, risk-consent dialog, `DiscordLoginWebView` dialog) moved into Settings → Accounts' Discord row (`VrcaSettingsPage.AccountsSection`). Tab order: identity header → status banner → alerts. **Post-Phase-7 fixes (user feedback round)**: (1) **Real-time friends count** — `publishFriendsOnline()` counts a friend online when their `status` OR `location` is non-blank/non-"offline"; the WS `friend-online`/`friend-location` payloads don't always carry `user.status` (the user object is optional), so the old status-only predicate never moved the count on live events — it only changed on the REST friends reload at reconnect ("count only updates when I reopen the app"). (2) **Banner ≠ pfp**: `VrcUserPresence.profilePicUrl` is now the round `userIcon` and the new `bannerUrl` carries `profilePicOverride` (the wide profile banner); the identity card renders the banner as the CARD BACKGROUND (session-authed `AsyncImage` + black vertical-gradient scrim 0.45→0.72; the trailing View-Profile/Sign-out IconButtons get a 0.40-alpha black circle backing when a banner is present). No banner (no VRChat+) → plain card. The admin-side `fetchProfilePicUrl`/`refreshProfilePic`/post-2FA pic capture all prefer `userIcon` and only fall back to `profilePicOverride` — cropping the banner into the avatar circle was the "banner used as pfp" bug. (3) **Trust rank chip follows VRChat's real names+colors** (`trustBadge()`; VRChat's tag names are OFFSET from the displayed ranks, same mapping as VRCX): basic→"New User" blue 0xFF1778FF, known→"User" green 0xFF2BCF5C, trusted→"Known User" orange 0xFFFF7B42, veteran→"Trusted User" purple (lightened 0xFFB18FE4 for dark-bg readability), legend→"Veteran" yellow 0xFFFFD000, unknown→"Visitor" grey; "Legendary" deliberately excluded (it does not exist). Badge container = `color.copy(alpha=0.22f)` with colored label text. `VrchatPipelineService.prettyTrustRank` (rank-change notifications) uses the same corrected mapping. (4) **Notifications section defaults EXPANDED**; its expand state lives in the process-scoped `AlertSectionState.expanded` (same pattern as `StatusBannerState`) — persists across tab switches, resets to expanded only on a fresh process; a headless OEM revival has no UI on screen, so the reset is only ever visible on a genuine reopen, which is exactly the intended behavior.
- **Login polish (Phase 7 same pass, `VrchatLoginScreen.kt`)**: the **VRChat platform-status warning** ("VRChat is having platform issues — login may fail, it's not you") now lives INSIDE `VrchatLoginScreen` (one-shot `fetchVrchatStatusWarning()` on entry + **re-fetch on a FAILED login attempt**), so ALL login surfaces get it — Settings takeover, VRChat-tab takeover, AND onboarding (whose own duplicate warning in `StepVrchatLogin` was removed since it embeds this screen). Explainer tightened to 2 lines; the 2FA step (both email + authenticator variants) adds "VRChat will remember this device — you won't be asked again." Resend-cooldown + segmented digit boxes (doc nice-to-haves) deliberately not built.
- **Media (Phase 6 revamp, `NowPlayingPage.kt`)**: Notification Access shrank to a **one-line error-tinted warning row shown ONLY while the permission is actually missing** (tap → settings; onboarding + Settings own the permission otherwise). **The gate is the REAL granted-permission check `NotificationManagerCompat.getEnabledListenerPackages(ctx).contains(ctx.packageName)` (re-checked on `ON_RESUME`), NOT `vm.listenerConnected`** — that flag only flips true once the OS (re)binds the `NotificationListenerService` and pushes a state update, so after a process restart it sits false even though access IS granted, which made this warning (and the Home `SetupHealthCard`'s `notifOk`, likewise switched to the real check) falsely persist while music was clearly being detected. A proper **Now Playing `CompactSectionCard`** (default expanded): summary = "Title — Artist · Source", trailing state chip (Idle/Source off/Ad/LIVE/Playing/Paused — `nowPlayingIsAd`/`nowPlayingIsLive` are now public read-only on the VM), expanded shows the **source app's launcher icon** (`rememberAppIcon` via PackageManager `getApplicationIcon(pkg).toBitmap()` — no network/deps, MusicNote fallback). **Package-visibility requirement (Android 11+/API 30+)**: `getApplicationIcon()` throws `NameNotFoundException` for a package the app can't SEE, and the app only gains *implicit* visibility to a media app while mid-interaction with its `MediaSession` — so the icon resolved only intermittently and otherwise fell back to the generic glyph (the "very inconsistent icon" bug). Fixed by a `<queries>` block in `AndroidManifest.xml` declaring the three known media packages (`com.spotify.music`, `com.google.android.youtube`, `com.google.android.apps.youtube.music`) PLUS an `<intent>` for `android.media.browse.MediaBrowserService` so arbitrary media apps ("other media apps are always allowed") resolve too. `rememberAppIcon` keys its `remember` on `pkg`, so with visibility now deterministic the single lookup succeeds and caches. Also: track + artist + source label, the **EXACT rendered chatbox lines** verbatim in monospace (new `vm.currentMusicChatboxLines()` accessor over the private `buildNowPlayingLines()`; re-read keyed on the page's 60ms `previewT` ticker so the bar/time advance), and **per-source enable rows** (Spotify / YouTube / YouTube Music). **Per-source enables**: `mediaSourceSpotify/Youtube/YtMusic` VM flags (default ON) ← DataStore keys `media_source_spotify/youtube/ytmusic` (`UserPreferencesRepository`), set via `vm.setMediaSourceFlag(pkg, enabled)` (package constants `VrcaViewModel.PKG_SPOTIFY/PKG_YOUTUBE/PKG_YTMUSIC`). The gate lives in `buildNowPlayingLines()`: `if (nowPlayingDetected && !isActiveMediaSourceEnabled()) return emptyList()` — a disabled source renders NOTHING in chatbox/preview while detection keeps running (re-enabling resumes instantly); unlisted media apps are always allowed. Local-only prefs (no Firestore sync, not in the feature-session restore). Progress-bar presets are **half-height `PresetRow`s** (name labelMedium + glyph on one line, 6dp vertical padding, highlight = selection, NO "Selected" label) inside a `CompactSectionCard` whose summary is the current preset name. The old "Detected / Preview" debug card is GONE — its raw Title/Artist lines joined the existing Listener block in Settings → Debug. **Post-Phase-6 fixes**: the Now Playing card AND the Progress-bar-style card both **default COLLAPSED and do NOT persist expansion** (`CompactSectionCard persistExpansion = false` — plain `remember` instead of `rememberSaveable`, so leaving the tab snaps them shut on return; per user request). The Progress-bar-style card gains a **"Show progress bar" `ToggleRow`** (`vm.musicShowProgress` / `setMusicShowProgressFlag` ← DataStore `music_show_progress`, default ON): OFF makes `buildNowPlayingLines()` return ONLY the title line (ads render just "Ad", the `● LIVE` marker is kept — it isn't a progress bar); the card summary reads "Hidden — title only" while off. **Ads NEVER lose the progress bar**: when the player reports no ad duration (some audio ads), the ad branch renders a static zero-position bar (no time suffix) instead of dropping the bar entirely (the old fallback). **"Filter Titles" `ToggleRow`** (Now Playing card → "Formatting" section, `vm.musicCleanTitles` / `setMusicCleanTitlesFlag` ← DataStore `music_clean_titles`, **default ON**, local-only, not synced): controls the `TitleCleaner` cruft-stripping + artist-dedup + one-line fitting. ON = `TitleCleaner.fitOneLine(...)` (the full ladder below). OFF = `TitleCleaner.rawOneLine(rawTitle, rawArtist, VRC_MAX_CHARS)` — combines `"artist — title"` verbatim with NO cleaning/fitting, only a hard 144-char cap so the chatbox protocol limit can't be exceeded; the line is free to WRAP in-game (the user opted out of shortening). Applies to BOTH the main music line and the `● LIVE` stream line. The char meter / preview reflect the chosen mode since both read `buildNowPlayingLines()` through the same gate.
- **Feature toggles live ONLY in Home's Quick Toggles.** The redundant per-feature enable switches were removed from the detail pages: the Pinned toggle and Cycle toggle in the Automations tab (`AutomationsPage.kt`), and the "Enable Now Playing block" toggle + the "Demo mode (testing)" toggle in the Media tab (`NowPlayingPage.kt`). The "Music demo mode" toggle in Settings → Interface and the demo toggle in the legacy `NowPlayingPage` (`LegacyPagesAndSettingsPage.kt`) were also removed. The `vm.setAfk/Cycle/SpotifyEnabledFlag` functions stay (driven by Quick Toggles); `spotifyDemoEnabled`/`setSpotifyDemoFlag` remain defined but now have no UI entry point (demo-track logic is effectively dormant). `NowPlayingPage` no longer takes `onPersistSpotifyEnabled`/`onPersistSpotifyDemo` params.
- Settings: Full page accessed via gear icon in top app bar. Contains Permissions, About, Help, and collapsible Debug section
- Admin: Full page accessed via gavel icon in top app bar (admin build only). Includes targeted APK push per user and release retraction.

### Admin UI (Phase 6 redesign)
The admin panel shares one visual language defined in **`AdminUiKit.kt`** — all admin surfaces compose from these primitives so density + clarity stay consistent:
- `AdminTone` (Neutral/Primary/Success/Warn/Error/Info) + `toneColors(tone)` map a semantic tone to M3 container/on/accent colors.
- `AdminSectionCard(title, icon, tone, trailing) { content }` — an `ElevatedCard` with an icon-in-tinted-circle header. `AdminCardHeader(...)` is the standalone header for use inside an existing card/column (used to re-skin the detail sections without restructuring their bodies).
- `AdminStatTile(label, value, icon, tone)` — big-number dashboard tile.
- `StatusPill(text, tone)` — rounded status chip (replaces raw `Badge`s for ONLINE/VRC/BAN/WARN).
- `AdminLabeledRow(label, value, mono, labelWidth)` — label→value row (ids monospaced).
- `AdminAvatar(name, online, size)` — a first-letter **initial circle** plus an online/offline status dot. **Profile pictures are NOT shown in the admin UI at all** (final design): they were never stored in Firestore (deliberate cost decision), and the on-demand VRChat+ fetch through the admin's own session (`VrchatAuthManager.fetchProfilePicUrl(userId)` + Coil via `VrchatImageLoader`) **never worked reliably in practice and was removed entirely per the user** — `fetchProfilePicUrl` and its per-id cache are deleted from `VrchatAuthManager`, and `AdminAvatar` no longer takes a `vrchatUserId` param. Do NOT re-add admin profile pictures. **Discord profile pictures are likewise not shown** — the admin app has no access to another person's Discord account, so the only source would have been a stored `discordAvatarUrl` (removed long ago), and Discord is not auth-fetchable by userId.

**Profile-picture data flow (NONE — admin shows initials only)**: Profile pictures are deliberately **never stored in or read from Firestore** (cost), and since the on-demand admin-session fetch was removed (it didn't work), the admin UI renders **name initials only**. **No `vrchatProfilePic` or `discordAvatarUrl` fields are written anywhere** — removed from `VrcaViewModel.buildUserSnapshot()` + `captureStateForSync()`, from `VrchatPipelineService.onOpen` (the one-shot prompt write) and `syncPresenceToFirestore()`, and from `DiscordRpcService` (the avatar capture + prompt write are gone). `UserRow`/`UserDetail`/`parseUserRow` no longer carry avatar URL fields. (`firestore.rules` `selfMutableKeys()` still *permits* `vrchatProfilePic`/`discordAvatarUrl` for backward-compat with older public clients that may still write them — the keys are simply ignored on read; nothing in the current app writes them. `currentAvatarThumbnailImageUrl` / `vrchatAvatarThumb`, the *worn avatar's* thumbnail, is unrelated and still synced for friend-change notifications.) The `VrchatAuthManager.refreshProfilePic()` / `getStoredProfilePic()` helpers remain but are no longer wired to any write. **`VrchatImageLoader` (`com.vrca.admin`, Coil + session-cookie interceptor) still exists** but its only consumer is the PUBLIC build's VRChat tab (the user's OWN avatar + profile banner, authorised by that device's own session — which works).

**Dashboard** (`DashboardTab.kt`): a 2×2 stat grid (Users/Online/Warned/Banned via `AdminStatTile`) plus a **unified "Recent activity" feed** (`ActivityEvent` list) that replaced the old redundant "Live overview" count hero. The feed merges (sorted by time, capped 40) from: **moderationEvents** (ban/unban/warn/remove_warn/**ban_evasion_detected**), **announcements** posted (live/draft), and **VRChat platform status** incidents (`status.vrchat.com/api/v2/summary.json` via OkHttp — free, not Firestore). `ActivityEvent` carries a serializable `ActivityKind` enum (icon + tone derived at render time) so the feed can be persisted. **Read cost is near-zero by design — `DashboardFeedCache` persists the feed to the admin device** (SharedPreferences `vrca_admin_dashboard`, JSON) AND in a process-level cache: (1) re-entering the tab within `FEED_CACHE_TTL_MS` (60s) costs **0 reads**; (2) otherwise the fetch is **incremental** — `whereGreaterThan("createdAt", cursor)` per source, so it reads only genuinely-new docs (≈1 read per source = ~2 total when nothing's new, Firestore's per-query minimum). The full ~33-doc read happens **once** (first run), then the cursors (`newestModMs`/`newestAnnMs`) and cached events persist across app restarts. The refresh button (`feedTick`) forces an incremental fetch. Each row is an iconed circle + title + subtitle + compact relative time.
**Directory** (`UsersTab.kt` list): rows show `AdminAvatar` (initials) + name/secondary + world + a pill row (ONLINE/VRC/BAN/WARN), the user's **app version** (`versionName`, shown as muted `labelSmall` next to the pills — written by the public app as `BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME`. **`versionName`/`versionCode` ride `captureStateForSync()` (hourly/edit delta) AND `buildLivePayload()` (watched 10s loop)** so the admin's version column updates reliably after an app UPDATE — previously they were written only by `buildUserSnapshot()` + the bootstrap cold-boot `safeUser` write (which runs on a clean swipe-reopen/fresh install and is throttled 20 min), so a user who only warm-resumed could show a STALE version indefinitely. Now it self-corrects within an hour (or ~10s if watched); zero extra Firestore writes (piggybacks on writes that already fire) and carried on `UserRow`/`parseUserRow`/`UsersDirectoryCache`), and relative `lastActiveAt`. Header has search, warned/banned filters, count, refresh, +500 paging.
**Detail — remote OSC Start/Stop + sending/instance-type indicators (`UsersTab.kt`, zero bonus reads/writes)**: the detail's "Live Chatbox Output" card shows a **SENDING / IDLE `StatusPill`** in its header = the user's master OSC Start/Stop gate (`oscSending`, whether the chatbox is ACTUALLY transmitting — distinct from the per-feature Pinned/Cycle/Music/Time chips, which only say what's configured), plus a **single full-width Start/Stop toggle button** (like Home — red "Stop sending" while sending, filled "Start sending" while idle; device-only). **Optimistic UI (`optimisticSending`)**: the pill AND the button flip INSTANTLY on press (the user's app applies the command instantly too) instead of waiting on the sync; the override then reconciles to the AUTHORITATIVE synced `oscSending` — it's cleared when the synced value catches up to the command (success) OR after a 30s timeout (command didn't land → self-heals back to the real value). Because `oscSending` rides the watched 10s loop / 30s edit debounce / hourly delta, a genuine desync always corrects on the user's next liveness write. Cost model: `oscSending` is synced by piggybacking on writes that already fire — added to `VrcaViewModel.captureStateForSync()` (hourly delta) AND `buildLivePayload()` (the watched 10s loop, so it's live in the detail view within 10s) — it is display-only, NOT in any apply path (`applyOfflineToggleEdits`/`applyRemoteConfig` never touch it), so it never clobbers local runtime state; the admin reads it via the existing 10s detail poll → **zero bonus reads/writes**. Remote Start/Stop is the ONLY cost: **one write per press** — a single-doc `oscCommand`(`"start"`/`"stop"`)+`oscCommandAt`(serverTimestamp) to THIS device's doc (device-only, NOT `applyAccountWide`), read by the user's EXISTING moderation snapshot listener (`lastOscCommandMs` dedup, fresh <60s) → calls `startSending()`/`stopSending()`. **Clock-skew tolerance (`CMD_SKEW_TOLERANCE_MS` = 2 min)**: the freshness gate compares the SERVER `oscCommandAt` timestamp to the device's local wall clock; a device clock lagging the server made `(now - ms)` NEGATIVE on the admin's write snapshot, so the old `in 0L..60_000L` gate SKIPPED the command and it only applied on the NEXT 10-30s poll once the clock advanced (the tester-found "Start/Stop only happens on their 10-30s poll sync" bug). The gate is now `in -CMD_SKEW_TOLERANCE_MS..60_000L` so a skewed-but-fresh command applies on the FIRST snapshot. The SAME negative-lower-bound tolerance was applied to the `killSignal`, `logoutVrchatAt`, and `logoutDiscordAt` gates (identical latent bug). An **instance-type chip** (Public/Friends/Friends+/Group/Invite/Invite+ via `StatusPill`) sits next to the `📍 world (N/cap)` line, derived locally by `instanceTypeLabel(vrchatLocation)` from the already-synced/already-read location string (`~friends(`/`~hidden(`/`~group(`/`~private(`/`~canRequestInvite` markers, same logic as the public `parseInstanceType`) — no VRChat call, no reads/writes; hidden when the location is blank/redacted ("private"). `firestore.rules`: `oscSending` in `selfMutableKeys()`, `oscCommandAt`/`oscCommand` in `ownerOnlyUserKeys()`. `UserDetail` carries `oscSending`.
**Detail** (`UsersTab.kt` detail): **revamped** — a "Back to directory" row above an identity card with a large (60dp) `AdminAvatar`, prominent name + secondary name, status pills, optional world line, an `AdminLabeledRow` facts block (VRChat id / authUid / device / **a single "last seen"** liveness field — the old separate active/updated/synced rows were collapsed into one, sourced from the live 10s detail poll), and a tidy two-up action footer (**Moderate** + **Kill App** side-by-side instead of stacked full-width). `UserDetail` carries the live liveness timestamps so they refresh during watching. Below it, `DetailBlock` renders iconed `AdminCardHeader` sections: VRChat presence, Live Chatbox Output + remote toggles, Pinned message + presets, Cycle (interval editor in the header trailing slot), App & Updates + targeted APK push, and moderation flags. All prior admin capabilities are preserved — presentation-only over the same action logic.
**Moderation / Announcements / Log tabs**: re-skinned with `AdminCardHeader` (Gavel/Warning/Block/History/Campaign) and `StatusPill`s. Moderation's target card shows an `AdminAvatar` + BANNED/WARNED/DEVICE BAN/CLEAN pills and `AdminLabeledRow` ids. Announcements rows show LIVE/DRAFT + priority pills.

**Public-build Discord button** (`VrcaScreen.kt`): on the **public** build the top-bar navigation slot (where the admin Gavel sits on the admin build) shows a button with the **real Discord logo** (`res/drawable/ic_discord.xml`, the official Clyde mark as a tinted `VectorDrawable` rendered via `painterResource` — Material Icons has no brand logos, so the old `Icons.Filled.Forum` speech-bubble was a placeholder) when `config/app.discordInvite` is a non-blank link — tapping opens the invite externally. The admin sets the link in `ConfigTab` ("Discord invite link" field, saved to `config/app.discordInvite`). Blank link → button hidden. **The invite value rides `VrcaApp`'s single `config/app` snapshot listener** (the SAME listener that reads the ToS `tosVersion`/`tosText`/`tosUrl`) and is passed into `VrcaScreen(discordInvite=…)` — the old separate one-shot `get()` in `VrcaScreen` was deleted, so the config doc is read ONCE, and the button is now LIVE (an admin editing the link updates it without a relaunch).

**Live-while-open reads (foreground-scoped listeners)**: the design rule for admin-pushed content is a snapshot listener SCOPED TO THE FOREGROUND (a Compose `DisposableEffect` — attaches while the screen is composed, detaches on background/leave via `onDispose`). Its first callback is ONE read (same as a one-shot `get()`); it bills again only when a change actually lands while you're looking, and ZERO while backgrounded — so a push fans out only to currently-OPEN users. This is CHEAPER than a process-scoped (always-on VM) listener, which stays attached backgrounded and bills reads for changes nobody's viewing. Process-scoped listeners are reserved for things that must act while the app is CLOSED (the moderation/ban/kill listener in the app-scoped VM). Applied to: **ToS + `discordInvite`** (`VrcaApp` `config/app` listener, one doc), **in-app announcements** (`VrcaScreen` — `announcements where active==true` `DisposableEffect` listener, replacing the old one-shot `get()`; the background-surviving `VrchatPipelineService.attachAnnouncementsListener` is still the source of the NOTIFICATION when off-screen), **targeted releases** (`releases/{deviceHash}` `DisposableEffect` listener, already live), and **global "release to all"** (`VrcaApp` — a `releases/latest` `DisposableEffect` listener that shows the forced `UpdateDialog` live, never DOWNGRADING a newer targeted release already shown; the 6h `startAppUpdateCheckLoop` service poll stays as the backgrounded catch-up).

### Firestore Sync Architecture
The sync model is intentionally minimal — Firestore costs money and we only push to it when we have to. Three classes of writes:

1. **App-open / cold-open write (one)**: After `initialDataLoaded` flips, `applyRemoteContentBeforeSync()` reads the Firestore doc to pick up any admin edits made while the user was offline (messages, presets, intervals — not toggles, which always start OFF), then `performSelfSync(coldOpen = true)` runs once with the merged state (`isOnlineInApp=true`, `lastActiveAt`). On first install or when `lastSyncedValues` is empty, this is a full ~40-field snapshot write (handles doc creation). On subsequent cold-opens with a persisted baseline, this is a **delta write**: only liveness fields + any content that changed since the previous session are written — unchanged presets/chatbox stay untouched. **Cold-open liveness throttle (`COLD_OPEN_LIVENESS_THROTTLE_MS` = 20 min)**: the background-survival stack (`START_STICKY`, the ~15-min `PipelineWatchdogWorker`, `BootReceiver`, OEM killers) resurrects the process repeatedly, and EACH resurrection re-ran this cold-open delta write (4 liveness fields) plus a self-listener echo read — that per-restart cost × frequent kills was the dominant *"steady Firestore reads/writes even with no admin browsing"* source (each cold start also re-attaches ~4-6 snapshot listeners, whose initial reads stack into the periodic read spikes). The cold-open write is now SKIPPED when (a) it's `coldOpen`, (b) the delta is liveness-ONLY (no content changed — gated on `LIVENESS_KEYS`), and (c) the last *actual* write (`PREF_LAST_SELF_SYNC_MS`, persisted via `markSelfSyncWritten()`) was within the throttle window. The still-fresh `lastActiveAt` already proves online, so status is unaffected; the **hourly heartbeat is anchored to the last real write** (first tick delay = `HOURLY - sinceLastWrite`, floored 60s) so a liveness write still lands within the 65-min staleness window even when the cold-open is skipped. A 15-min churn cadence drops from ~4 writes/hr to ~2/hr; a pathological fast (<20 min) restart loop writes at most once per ~20-40 min instead of once per restart. The first-ever sync and any content delta are NEVER throttled. **`clearSwipedAway` (called from `MainActivity.onCreate` on a real user reopen) also clears `PREF_LAST_SELF_SYNC_MS`** so the throttle can never suppress the cold-open write after a swipe — the stale `offlineAt` on the doc MUST be overridden by a fresh `lastActiveAt` or the user stays offline in the directory.
2. **Hourly liveness + delta write (periodic, the ONLY steady-state periodic write)**: `VrcaViewModel.startHourlyHeartbeat()` fires once per hour after the cold-open write. Each tick calls `performSelfSync()` which always writes liveness (`lastActiveAt`, `isOnlineInApp`, `lastSeenAt`, `updatedAt`) and includes only content fields that changed since the last write (field-by-field delta comparison against `lastSyncedValues`). **The delta map (`captureStateForSync`) also carries the volatile preview/nowPlaying fields and the VRChat presence snapshot** (see schema notes above), so an unwatched user's directory row (preview text, current track, VRChat location) refreshes at hourly granularity for free — same single write, more fields. If nothing changed, only the 4 liveness fields are written — a single minimal merge. It is an **in-process coroutine**, NOT an AlarmManager wakeup: a swiped/OS-killed app stops firing it → goes stale → correctly counted offline.
3. **App-close write (one)**: `VrchatPipelineService.onTaskRemoved` / `AppShutdown` writes `isOnlineInApp=false, offlineAt, lastSeenAt` plus current VRChat presence fields if available. `VrcaViewModel.onCleared` writes `isOnlineInApp=false, offlineAt, lastSeenAt` via `GlobalScope` as a fallback for cases where the foreground service isn't running. The `offlineAt` server timestamp is the authoritative clean-shutdown marker the admin reads for instant swipe→offline (see "Admin online detection").
4. **Edits use a 30s idle-debounce write**: changing a toggle/message/preset persists to local DataStore immediately (as always) AND schedules ONE delta write 30s after the last edit via `startSelfSyncLoopIfNeeded()` (`SELF_SYNC_DEBOUNCE_MS = 30_000`). Each edit cancels+reschedules, so a burst of edits flushes exactly once. (This was briefly a true no-op during the Phase-1 cost reduction, but that made "edit a preset then close the app" revert on reopen — the app-open read overwrote the not-yet-synced edit — so the debounced write was restored in commit `e21103e`. `performSelfSync` writes only changed content + liveness, so an idle user who isn't editing schedules nothing. The call sites fire ONLY on genuine content/toggle changes, never on NowPlaying ticks or preview rebuilds.) Still pushed on the next hourly tick too (or instantly via the 10s live loop if an admin is watching). **Note: this means an actively-editing user, or a content change pulled in by cross-device sync, costs one write per 30s-quiet-period — it is NOT zero per-edit.**

**Hourly liveness model (Phase 1)**: Liveness is determined by `lastActiveAt`, written once per hour by `VrcaViewModel.startHourlyHeartbeat()` (anchored to cold-open) and every 10s while an admin watches this user. Admin online = `lastActiveAt` within ~65 min (or ~25s for a watched user). `lastSeenAt` is written as a legacy mirror (same timestamp) so older admin builds keep working. **Removed in Phase 1**: the 30s browse-gated heartbeat, the 5-min always-on heartbeat, the per-edit debounced self-sync, and the browse-volatile 30s loop — none of these write to Firestore anymore. An admin merely browsing the directory causes **zero** per-user writes; only opening a specific user's detail (which flips `AdminWatchState.isWatched`) starts that one user's 10s live loop.

**Browse heartbeat (FULLY REMOVED)**: Previously, when an admin was on the Dashboard/Users tab the admin app wrote `config/adminPresence.browsingAt` every 30s and public apps ran a 30s `lastSeenAt` heartbeat while that was fresh. Phase 1 made the public-side write a no-op but the admin still WROTE `browsingAt` and every public app still held a `config/adminPresence` snapshot listener feeding `AdminBrowsingState`. That listener was the single biggest admin-side read cost: every 30s `browsingAt` write fanned a billed read out to EVERY online user's listener (~120k reads/hour at 1k users) for ZERO benefit (nothing reads `AdminBrowsingState` to drive a write under the hourly model — `startBrowseVolatileSyncWatcher` is empty). Now **both sides are gone**: `AdminRuntime` no longer writes `browsingAt` (its loop only runs the local force-kill staleness sweep), and `VrchatPipelineService` no longer attaches the `config/adminPresence` listener. (`AdminBrowsingState` remains as dead code, unfed.)

**Slow always-on lastSeenAt heartbeat (REMOVED in Phase 1 — historical)**: The service used to write `lastSeenAt` every 5 minutes (`LASTSEEN_HEARTBEAT_MS`) while alive (~288 writes/user/day). This is replaced by the once-per-hour `lastActiveAt` write (~24 writes/user/day) owned by the ViewModel. The constant and `writeHeartbeat()` are deleted.

**Live-mode (watcher-gated)**: When an admin opens a specific user's detail page in the admin panel, the admin app refreshes `watcherActiveAt` every ~30s on that user's doc. The user app's snapshot listener feeds the timestamp into `AdminWatchState`; if fresh (within 60s), `isWatched` flips to true and two side-effects start:
- `VrcaViewModel.startLiveSyncWatcher` writes `buildLivePayload` (nowPlaying, preview, the feature toggles `afkEnabled`/`cycleEnabled`/`spotifyEnabled`/`timeEnabled`, `lastReportedTime`, `lastActiveAt`, `lastSeenAt`) every 10s (`LIVE_SYNC_INTERVAL_MS`). **`lastActiveAt` is included** so a watched user's liveness stays fresh in the admin directory (not just the detail view) — previously only `lastSeenAt` was written, and since `isUserOnline` only falls back to `lastSeenAt` when `lastActiveAt` is null, the directory could show a watched user as offline. **The toggles stream here so the admin detail's Pinned/Cycle/Music/Time button states reflect a user's own toggle change within 10s while watching** (they also persist via the hourly delta + the 30s edit debounce — `captureStateForSync` carries them — so an unwatched toggle surfaces on the next hourly write)
- `VrchatPipelineService.startPresenceRefreshLoop` writes VRChat presence every 10s

`VrcaViewModel.startBrowseVolatileSyncWatcher` is now a **no-op** (Phase 1): an admin merely browsing the directory no longer causes this user to emit any writes. The directory is populated from the admin's own one-shot fetch; only opening this user's detail (which flips `isWatched`) starts the 10s live loop.

The live loop stops instantly when `isWatched` flips back to false (`collectLatest` cancels the inner loop). When nobody is watching, neither loop touches Firestore — the only periodic write is the hourly `lastActiveAt`.

**Force-kill detection (hourly model)**: If the user's app is force-killed, neither `onTaskRemoved` nor `onCleared` fires, so `isOnlineInApp` stays `true` in Firestore — but the in-process hourly loop stops, so `lastActiveAt` goes stale. The admin determines offline by `lastActiveAt` age (>~65 min, or ~25s for a currently-watched user whose 10s loop stopped). The admin-side staleness sweep (`AdminRuntime`) keys on `lastActiveMs` (canonical `lastActiveAt`, falling back to `lastSeenAt`) with `ONLINE_STALENESS_WINDOW_MS = 65 min` — past the 60-min hourly heartbeat with 5 min of grace, so a live unwatched user is never false-flagged. When an admin is browsing and a user's `lastActiveAt` exceeds the window, the sweep writes `isOnlineInApp=false` to that user's doc.

**Admin kill switch**: Admin force-quits a user's app via "Kill App" (UsersTab) → account-wide batch writes **`killSignal` + `offlineAt` + `isOnlineInApp=false`** (all `serverTimestamp()`/false in the SAME `applyAccountWide` write — one write, no extra cost). The user's moderation listener reads `killSignal` and calls **`AppShutdown.killNowNoWrite(app)`** — NOT a bare `Process.killProcess`. A bare killProcess only BOUNCED the app: the four `START_STICKY` services + the `PipelineWatchdogWorker` promptly revived it. `killNowNoWrite` mirrors the swipe shutdown's guards (stamps `manual_kill_at` + `swiped_away`, cancels the watchdog, disarms `FeatureSessionStore`, stops the services, then `killProcess`+`exitProcess`) so the process STAYS dead — and the dying app still writes **zero** Firestore (the offline marker is written by the ADMIN in the kill batch, which is guaranteed to land, instead of relying on the dying process). A real user reopen clears the swipe flag (MainActivity) so the app works normally again — a kill is a force-quit, not a ban.
  - **One-shot per kill (the "reopen closes again a few times" fix)**: `killSignal` stays fresh (<60s) and is NEVER cleared, and the old `killSignalHandled` guard is IN-MEMORY (resets each process) — so a reopen within 60s re-read the same fresh signal and re-killed, bouncing repeatedly until it aged out. `handleAdminKill(killMs)` now persists **`last_handled_kill_ms`** (`vrca_remote` prefs, `commit()`) and only kills when `killMs > last_handled` (plus the ≤60s age window that blocks an ANCIENT signal on reinstall). A reopen sees the same `killMs` → skips; a genuinely NEW kill (newer serverTimestamp) still fires.
  - **Prompt + durable offline with a 7s reopen grace**: the kill batch's **`offlineAt`** is the durable marker — `isUserOnline`'s `offlineAt > lastActiveAt` flips the row offline immediately and keeps it there until the user actually reopens with a newer `lastActiveAt` (works whether or not the admin is watching; the incremental directory query already keys on `offlineAt`, and the 10s detail poll reads it, so zero extra reads). The **`killSignal`-scoped grace** (`KILL_GRACE_MS = 7s`) adds `if (killSignal within 7s of now) → offline` to `isUserOnline`, so a reopen within 7s can't instantly bounce the row back online (kill-scoped, so a normal swipe→instant-reopen still shows online right away). `killSignal` is carried on `UserRow`/`UserDetail`/`parseUserRow`/`UsersDirectoryCache`/the detail merge/`onUpdateUserRow` (all from the already-read snapshot — no new field written by the client, no extra reads). **Residual edge** (no worse than a normal OS force-kill): if the admin kills in the exact sub-second the user is cold-opening AND the user never reopens AND nobody's watching, the racing `lastActiveAt` can read newer than `offlineAt` → shows online until the 65-min staleness decay (or ~45s watched-stale). Deliberately NOT closing this with a tolerance on `offlineAt > lastActiveAt` — any tolerance wide enough to catch the race also risks pinning a genuine quick-reopen offline until the next hourly write.

**Single-session HARD-DENY (multi-device, same VRChat account) — replaced the old take-over**: only ONE device may use VRC-A per VRChat account. The lock doc `accounts/{vrchatUserId}` = `{ activeDevice, activeSince }` is now enforced as a **hard deny** (the previous newest-wins "take-over" + "Use here" reclaim was REMOVED). `VrcaViewModel.startAccountLockWatcher()` (re-attached per login via `presenceFlow`) reads/listens the lock: when it's free/ours → **claim** it (`claimAccount`, write `activeDevice=myHash`); when another device holds it → **`accountDenied=true`** → OSC blocked at the shared chokepoint (`refreshOscBlockGate` ORs it with `vrchatLoggedOut`/`forceUpdatePending`/`notActiveDevice→accountDenied`), Discord RPC disconnected locally (`disconnectDiscordLocally`: clears `discord_rpc_enabled`/`discord_session_seeded` + wipes WebView cookies + stops the service so it can't auto-restart), and `VrcaScreen` shows **`AccountDeniedScreen`** — a reassuring "account already registered, contact support" message (NOT scary "signed in on another device" wording) with a **"Join the support server"** button (the `config/app.discordInvite` link). The VRChat session + the lock LISTENER stay attached, so recovery is **automatic with no reopen**: when the holder signs out or an admin frees the lock (next snapshot absent), this device claims it and clears the deny. **Claiming now happens on EVERY login** (not just multi-device) so the next device sees a lock to be denied by — single-device users write one lock doc on login (the old "single-device never touches accounts" optimization is gone, traded for the deny guarantee). **Escape hatches (the only two)**: (1) the holder **properly signs out** of VRChat → `loggedOutSignal` → `releaseAccountLock()` deletes `accounts/{vrchatUserId}`; (2) **admin remote-logout** (below).
